Art. 66 of the Constitution states that an employee has the right to work-free days and annual paid leave specified in the Act. Other things are also defined, such as: maximum working time standards. The way in which these days are defined is laid down by the law. That's what we can read in the Constitution. What does that mean in practice? Art. 66 The Constitution regulates one of the fundamental rights of workers, which is the right to rest. This right includes a guarantee of statutory holidays and annual paid holidays. The right to rest in accordance with this provision of the Constitution shall be conferred on the staff member. The term is based on Article. Article 66 (1) (b) and (c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 2 of the Constitution is autonomous and includes not only a person who is in a job relationship but also any other person doing work for profit. The statutory guarantees of the right to rest should also cover persons providing work on a different basis than the contract of employment, which de lege summers in the legal system in force in Poland. Ladies and gentlemen, we must admit something that puts us, the legislators, in a bad light. To this day we have allowed, contrary to the provisions of the Constitution, the situation of unequal treatment of citizens depending on whether they are employed by the employer or, as the vast majority, they are so called. self-employment. We are not talking about huge entrepreneurs, we are often talking about people who were forced to switch to self-employment, because their main employer thought it was so convenient that it is a way of building for him less costs, that it is a formula more comfortable For him and not for the employee From the point of view of his performance of his duties. Art. 66 of the Constitution imposes responsibilities above all... And that's what we need to remember, because the basic law is an obligation imposed on the state. The main of these or one of the basic duties is to determine by law the content and manner in which the rights set out in this provision are implemented, including the rights and obligations arising from the right to leave, the right to rest. Ladies and gentlemen, leave is a social benefit due to the fact that you remain in employment. The Constitutional Tribunal, which has expressed its views on this matter, and the caselaw of the courts which have affected this case in many respects, states very clearly: the right to annual leave, i.e. within a year, it's an unconditional law. And this is the right assigned to the employee, regardless of how he is employed. Art. 66 of the Constitution refers to a law which sets out the content of the rights set out in that provision and how they are implemented. So it was the legislator's duty to clarify this. In material terms, this provision requires that statutory provisions be made in such a way that this right, the right to rest, can be exercised. It is therefore possible to speak of a certain charge of unconstitutionality in the context of the regulations in force at this time, which de facto deprive such a large group of citizens of their right to rest. Ladies and gentlemen, what are we talking about in practice? We must remember that in Poland the number of so-called. micro-entrepreneurs, people like that, that's $2 million, $80,000. Of this group, about 1 million, 300 000 people These are self-employed persons self-employment. These are not entrepreneurs employing other employees. These are people who generally perform certain duties on their own, as I said, often in companies in which there is another employee next to them, employed on the basis of a contract of employment. And imagine the situation: the one who is employed for a contract of employment has the right to paid leave, and the one who is on so-called leave. Self-employment, that right is deprived. This bill is a clear injustice for 1 million 300 thousand Poles and Poles we correct. Article 4 is replaced by the following: 66 of the constitution, which grants workers the right to rest, must now be implemented by us in the form of legal clarification. What are we designing? The right to rest should be granted not only to employees but also to self-employed workers who do not employ any employee under an employment contract. Employers who employ other employees would be excluded from the scope of the proposed solution. The essence of the changes that we propose, the essence of the amendment of the Law of Businessmen, is the granting of the so-called Law. self-employed relief in the payment of social security contributions in one month of the calendar year in respect of the exercise of the right to rest. In the current legal situation, as I said, there are no tools to implement the right of self-employed to rest. Vacation as such is an institution of labour law, but the status of the so-called. the self-employed is not regulated by these rules. In view of this, the only rational solution that can help to realise the right to rest of this group of people is to create a tool that would reduce the cost of so-called. self-employment at a time when such a person is not engaged in activities due to the fact that he is resting. The proposed amendment will remove the gap, as the lawyers say, from the legal system, in the axiological gap in this area. Ladies and gentlemen, what do we propose? We propose that in the new article that we attach to this regulation, i.e. in Article 4, paragraph 1 is replaced by the following: 18a, grant to self-employed persons who carry out business on a continuous basis for a period exceeding 6 months the possibility to benefit in a given calendar year in one month of their choice from a 50% reduction in compulsory social security contributions for the exercise of their right to rest. The reduction of the contribution will not affect the basis for the assessment of the contributions. So-called. The self-employed who will benefit from the relief will not suffer the consequences in the future, his pension will not be lower. The contribution to compulsory social security to the full amount will be financed by the state budget through the Social Insurance Institution. The premium shall be reduced by half in one month. To put it graphically, we provide a 2-week rest with a paid insurance premium. In view of the state of public finances, we propose that the proposed solution be applied only to low-income people. The beneficiaries of the relief would only be persons whose income from non-agricultural economic activity from the 6 months preceding the month of notification to the Social Security Office was no more than PLN 60 thousand. Please note that as authors, we are limiting ourselves to the designers of this project. We say this is not for all. This will not be for those who are on contracts with a very high wage, where we are not dealing with an employee, but from the formal point of view, we can say that a self-employed person who, under certain contracts, earns significant funds, exceeding the limit of PLN 60 thousand, which we propose. We offer this relief to the low and medium earners. At the same time, entrepreneurs who fulfil the conditions laid down in the article on social insurance shall be excluded from the catalogue of beneficiaries. It's about people who walk away. agricultural contributions. An appropriate notification of these persons will be a condition for exercising the right. ParlaMint-PL_2021-02-17-senat-21-1.u292	Thank you very much, Marshal. High Izbo! Minister! Ladies and gentlemen! On behalf of the Extraordinary Commission for Climate Affairs, the Committee on Local Government and State Administration, the Legislative Commission and the Committee on the Environment, I have the honour to present a report on the draft law amending the Environmental Law, Senate Printing No 286. The draft law deals with changing the compositions of the supervisory boards of voivodship funds for environmental protection and water management, how these supervisory boards are selected and how the management boards of voivodship funds for environmental protection and water management are selected. Currently, the supervisory boards of these institutions are composed of 5 members of four basically elected by the government, and one is indicated by the local government. I'll remind you, Art. 400. This is chapter 4 of the Act, which states that voivodship funds for environmental protection and water management, hereinafter referred to as funds, are local legal persons within the meaning of Article. Paragraph 9 (14) of the Act referred to in paragraph 9. 1. As a matter of fact, many years ago, I was the author of the bill. When special-purpose funds were liquidated in Poland, also municipal, district funds, and such funds existed... The provincial fund was a special-purpose fund and the national one too. We've created a new legal structure. On the basis of this new construction, municipal funds became part of the municipalities' budgets With funding for environmental protection. The same has happened in the case of district funds. A slightly different character and rank we gave to the regional environmental funds. As I recall, they had the status of a local legal person. From the beginning to the end, we wanted them to be under the supervision of local governments of voivodships. We were not afraid of decentralisation, we wanted to have a partner in the form of self-governments to carry out many environmental tasks, while maintaining oversight on the part of the government, government administration over the National Fund for Environmental Protection and Water Management, which is such a record, the article is a state legal person. We consciously distinguished the local legal person and the state legal person so as not to allow the minister to take control of the regional environmental funds in the future. However, we did not think, at all, that it was possible to take control of supervisory boards through a simple procedure related to the amendment of the law. Moreover, if the Marshals, the voivodships did not want to carry out, carry out the process of change, then it was assigned, these intentions In a bad way, I would like to say it clearly, bad for the state and bad for the power of the state. The minister responsible for climate and environment today, because he exercises such supervision and has these powers. I would also like to make it clear: where the government simply does not cope, it is still cynically passing on tasks to local governments. And, for example, removes opportunities for local authorities to operate in an area related to environmental protection and gives them clear responsibility, even in the air protection part. Because who in Poland is responsible for air protection? Yesterday, Minister Kurtyka, Minister of Environment and Climate at the meeting of the combined committees of national economy and environment, said unambiguously: local governments. Not the government of self-government. So, in fact, the local authorities were taken away with the instruments of responsibility and funding in this area, but the responsibility was left behind. More, that's what we're talking about. The government does not want to be held responsible for matters affecting this area. Of course, I clearly state that the local government has certain powers in this matter; there is even an article. Article 14 (1) (b) and (c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 1 in the Environmental Protection Act, applied in the voivodship dimension, i.e. in the part concerning the local government of the voivodship are assigned tasks related to environmental protection. It is incomprehensible that local governments were deprived of supervision of these local government legal entities. This bill restores this supervision, restores the possibility for local governments of voivodships to influence the voivodship's environmental protection funds, more, causes this supervision to be socialized, because the supervisory board will be composed of representatives of two communities, i.e. environmental NGOs and employers' organisations will have one representative on this supervisory board. It is really worth engaging employers' environments in environmental measures because they have a huge impact on activities in this area. In order not to extend this part on the report submitted on behalf of the Joint Committees, I would also like to make it clear that all the organisations concerned ♪ maybe with one exception ♪ They expressed a positive position and supported this solution, i.e. representatives of non-governmental organisations, all without exception, representatives of employers' organisations, including all without exception, local co-operations, all without exception. They all expressed a clear positive opinion on this draft law. There is one negative opinion, and that is the opinion of the government. I absolutely agree with this and propose that this amendment be adopted. In the latter part, it already has a technical dimension. ParlaMint-PL_2021-02-17-senat-21-1.u327	Mr. Marshal! Ladies and gentlemen of the Senators! Ladies and gentlemen! I would like to present a report by the Legislative Commission and the Committee on Human Rights, the Law and Petitions on the draft law amending the Law on State Compensation for Victims of Certain Offences, the Civil Procedure Code and the Code of Criminal Procedure, contained in print No 91. The Senate Marshal sent a letter to the Legislative Commission in March last year together with a draft law to consider it at first reading. The committees have completed their work in joint meetings. We started on 25 June 2020 and ended on the beginning of this year; this was also related to the request for detailed financial data. At the meeting on 29 January this year, we examined the draft law presented by the applicants at first reading, amended it and requested that the Senate adopt a single attached draft amending the law on state compensation for victims of certain criminal offences. Ladies and gentlemen, sometimes it is so that in the legal order the rules are actually dead. A law that is so rare that even using words that we have created a symbolic opportunity for a citizen is a profound, gross abuse. Ladies and gentlemen, we can say that until then, the compensation institution in 2014 and 2018 was used in such a way that the total amount of compensation granted did not exceed 365 thousand PLN throughout Poland over the four years. Ladies and gentlemen, there is no mistake here. It is 365 thousand PLN in the years 2014-2018. So much has been paid in accordance with the provisions in force so far in the context of compensation for criminal offences. It should also be pointed out that the legislator seemed to have noticed this problem because in 2015 these provisions were amended. Even then, it was diagnosed that this institution was rarely used. Yes, the compensation institution was used very rarely. Despite this revision of the 2015 legislation, we were able to see this by looking at the data In the following years, it was still rarely used. This was despite the fact that the 2015 amendment raised the upper limit of the amount of compensation, deleted the rules which ruled it out in certain cases of remission and allowed compensation to be granted in the event of non-deception of the perpetrator of the prohibited act. Significantly, the victim was exempted from the costs of civil court proceedings by new rules. You can ask why, then. Despite the fact that this is possible in the legal system, although the provisions have already been amended in order to make greater use of these compensations In practice, this institution does not continue to function. The answer to this question is that, at present, compensations are included in the budgets of the courts in the same pool as administrative resources such as training. This causes that in some cases It's hard not to get that impression. The judges must adjust the amount of benefits to the low level of resources remaining after the other administrative tasks have been carried out. Of course, the second element is too rarely effectively informing people who would be entitled to compensation that this possibility exists. Ladies and gentlemen, I hope that this project will effectively address these problems. What do we propose in the revision of the legislation related to the initiative with which the Senate Legislative Commission comes? In order to increase the effectiveness of compensation institutions, we propose, firstly, that maximum time limits be set for courts to recognise cases. However, the compensation case should be settled at first instance no later than 6 months after the date of the application for compensation to the court and the appeal should be heard within 3 months from the date of its submission. Secondly, we point out that the compensation will be paid by the Minister of Justice through a regional court within one month of the date of the decision. The key change, ladies and gentlemen, is that compensation will be paid from the Fund for Hurted Aid and Post-Penitentiary Assistance, commonly referred to as the Fund of Justice. The district court shall keep separate records of the compensation paid. Thirdly, we extend the possibility to claim an advance on compensation by the amount of earnings or other maintenance costs lost. Fourthly, we introduce an obligation to inform the victims of the offence of the possibility of applying for a security against state compensation. Ladies and gentlemen, the proposed changes in our assessment have the opportunity to effectively eliminate those elements of the existing system, which in practice make them dead records. This view also seems to be shared by the actors and institutions to which we have requested an opinion on the proposed amendments to the legislation. As regards the opinions which were sent to the committee, the Minister of Justice welcomed the direction of the action taken, in particular as regards the dissemination in society of knowledge about the possibilities for applying the solutions envisaged in the project. He also expressed some doubts about some of the elements contained in the proposal, but I think that we addressed them clearly and clearly at the committee meeting. The project was also fully supported I'm referring to opinions here. By the Foundation for the Assistance of Victims of Crimes and by the Supreme Court, who expressed the view that it is reasonable to propose that the court hearing compensation should hear the case for granting it no later than within 6 months and that it would be advisable to fix a time limit for a hearing of the appeal at the level of 3 months. In the Supreme Court's view, there are no doubts as to the transitional provisions. In turn, the Helsinki Foundation for Human Rights assessed that the proposed changes were going in the right direction. The Ombudsman welcomes the intention to speed up the procedure for determining compensation for victims of crime. The initiative itself was an initiative for which we were inspired by the Ombudsman's speech. When making law in parliament, we should always remember that the rule of law is to be formulated so that it is clear, legible and to be applied in practice. Today we can say that the rules and regulations, as well as the practice of their operation in the context of compensation for victims of certain criminal offences, are so formulated that compensation is granted but only formally. The aim of this project is to ensure that compensation is granted to citizens in a real way. I am very cordially asking on behalf of the Joint Committees to adopt this extremely necessary project. Thank you very much.

The first reading of the draft took place on 29 January 2021 at the meeting of the Joint Committees, which I had just mentioned. The purpose of the Act is to extend the list of grounds contained in Chapter 58 of the Code of Criminal Procedure, for which the right to claim compensation and/or compensation from the Treasury for undoubtedly incorrect accusations, the application of a protective measure and prosecution, as well as the extension of the limitation period to 3 years of the possibility of claiming compensation or compensation. If there is any situation in which a person who pursues his rights under this law would be exposed to any repression or even negative consequences, or we would find that there is such a systemic problem, then the Polish diplomatic services and the Ministry of Foreign Affairs will certainly support such persons. On the second point, the Ministry of Foreign Affairs did not carry out such analyses. I do not know whether it was carried out by the Legislative Commission or by the authors of the project, but we do not have such analyses.
